% ==================================================================
%  Monte Carlo flights in perspective -- one sigconf text width (8.2 cm),
%  pdfLaTeX, plain TikZ (no pgfplots; coordinates are embedded)
%  Data:      cpp2lean sim/out/montecarlo/runs/<lean|ref>-<i>.csv.gz and summary.csv
%             (sim/montecarlo.py): 200 starts uniform in the takeoff box A with a
%             uniform heading, each
%             flown by copter_lean (the Lean translations of ArduPilot's classes)
%             and copter_ref (ArduPilot's classes compiled unmodified) on the 1 m/s
%             figure-eight course with the tuned gains; the two vehicles' positions
%             are equal on every tick (checked by the script), so one set of 200 is
%             drawn; the contract holds on 400 of 400 runs.
%  Camera:    render_overview.py's (yaw 17, pitch -34, distance 10.2 m, field of
%             view 34 deg, aspect 1.5), projected by the script; floor grid of
%             0.5 m, the ceiling's near edge at 2 m, the corridor as the planned path
%             (takeoff point, w0..w7) stroked at the apparent diameter of a 0.25 m
%             cylinder (in 0.25 m pieces, so it narrows with depth), A in ochre,
%             the trajectories thinned to 150 points (largest-triangle-three-buckets).
%  Inset:     the takeoff region (east and north (-0.3400000000000001, 0.3400000000000001) m, up (0.0, 0.9) m)
%             through the same camera, enlarged 5.9 times; the climbs from tick 0 to
%             0.25 s after reaching w0 (w0 reached at 1.3675 to 1.6900 s), 120 points each, and
%             the starts as dots with a 3 cm tick toward the initial heading (uniform in
%             [-180, 180) deg, heading_rad of summary.csv).
%  Spread:    (not drawn) at each tick the largest distance over the runs
%             from the run's position to the nominal flown path (sim/out/lean-course-1ms,
%             the ground start at the origin, in A), log scale, 0 to 6 s. Initial
%             19.74 cm; first below 1 cm at 1.9550 s (below for good from 2.7100 s),
%             first below 1 mm at 5.7550 s (below from 5.7550 s, up to the final hover).
%             Largest distance between two starts 44.78 cm. The distance between
%             runs at the same tick does not shrink so: they reach w0 up to
%             0.32 s apart and then fly the same path shifted in time.
%  Generated by cpp2lean sim/figs/make_montecarlo_3d.py; no hand-edited coordinates.
% ==================================================================
\definecolor{fwInk}{HTML}{22252A}%
\definecolor{fwMute}{HTML}{8A8F96}%
\definecolor{fwHair}{HTML}{C9CCD1}%
\definecolor{fwOchre}{HTML}{C4860F}%
\definecolor{fwBlue}{HTML}{1E3F7C}%
\definecolor{fwVerm}{HTML}{C0412B}%
\definecolor{fwSage}{HTML}{C3D7B4}%
\begin{tikzpicture}[
  x=1cm, y=1cm, line cap=round, line join=round,
  tick/.style={font=\scriptsize, text=fwMute, inner sep=1pt},
  lab/.style={font=\scriptsize, text=fwInk!85, inner sep=1pt},
  grid/.style={draw=fwHair!70, line width=0.25pt},
  ceil/.style={draw=fwBlue!45, line width=0.35pt, dash pattern=on 1.6pt off 1.2pt},
  coredge/.style={draw=fwBlue!40},
  corfill/.style={draw=fwBlue!8},
  run/.style={draw=fwBlue, line width=0.25pt, draw opacity=0.10},
  Afill/.style={fill=fwOchre, fill opacity=0.18},
  Aedge/.style={draw=fwOchre, line width=0.35pt},
  st/.style={fill=fwOchre, draw=none},
  hd/.style={draw=fwOchre!80!black, line width=0.3pt, draw opacity=0.75},
  wp/.style={draw=fwInk!70, fill=white, line width=0.35pt},
  frame/.style={draw=fwMute, line width=0.35pt},
  axis/.style={draw=fwHair, line width=0.4pt},
  mk/.style={draw=fwHair, line width=0.4pt},
  spr/.style={draw=fwBlue, line width=0.6pt},
  ref/.style={draw=fwMute, line width=0.3pt, dash pattern=on 1.2pt off 1pt},
]

% Suggested caption: The 200 Monte Carlo flights in perspective, through the overview's camera. Every start
% lies in the takeoff box A, with a uniform heading (ticks in the inset); every trajectory stays in the
